DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player taps to split an ever‑expanding pulse ring into two concentric waves, threading them through nested obstacles and re‑uniting them for a satisfying score boost.

## Player
The player controls a simple tap cursor that initiates a split of the current pulse. No movement; the only verb is **tap** to split, and optionally **hold** to time the recombination.

## Core loop
Every few seconds a pulse expands outward. The player watches the ring approach obstacles, decides the optimal moment to tap, splits the pulse, guides the two inner rings through gaps, and then times their recombination for extra points. Successive splits keep the rhythm flowing and raise tension.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Nested barriers**: stationary concentric rings with apertures that only one of the two split pulses can pass through at a time.
- **Rotating arcs**: arcs that sweep around the center, temporarily blocking a path and forcing the player to choose a different split timing.
- **Pulsing walls**: walls that expand and contract, creating windows of opportunity that must be timed precisely.
- **Dynamic spikes**: short‑lived spikes that shoot outward from the edge; they disappear after a beat, encouraging quick splits.

## Score
- **Recombine**: when the two split pulses merge back together after successfully navigating obstacles.
- **Perfect timing**: tapping exactly as the pulse touches an aperture grants a bonus multiplier.
- **Chain**: consecutive successful recombinations without a miss increase a combo counter that adds extra points.

## Win and lose
- **Win**: reach a predefined number of successful recombinations or survive a set number of pulse cycles; the screen flashes and the pulse steadies into a luminous final bloom.
- **Lose**: any split pulse collides with a solid obstacle or fails to recombine before the pulse dissipates; the ring fractures and fades, accompanied by a brief ripple of darkness.

## Feel
A steady, hypnotic rhythm punctuated by sharp, tactile moments of split and recombine. Visuals favor a calm neon glow against a dark backdrop, with smooth gradients and subtle particle trails. Audio consists of soft ambient drones punctuated by clear, resonant chimes on each split and a satisfying crescendo when pulses merge.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of slicing a perfect expanding ring, threading the halves through tight gaps, and hearing the satisfying click as they reunite.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold + meta.json — Create index.html with header, description, controls list, canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md and add matching meta.json stub; include empty <script> tag.
2. Game state objects — Define player, hazards, score, and phase objects as plain literals with initial values at the top of the script.
3. Animation loop — Implement startLoop() using requestAnimationFrame, include cancelAnimationFrame handling, wrap update/render calls in try/catch, and clear the canvas each frame.
4. Input handling — Set up a `keys` Set with keydown/keyup listeners, add R-key restart hook and Restart button click handler that both invoke a reset() function.
5. Player movement — Add dt‑based physics for the player using keys input, enforce max speed, friction, and clamp position within canvas bounds; ensure meta.json controls affect movement.
6. Hazard system — Create hazard data structure, spawn logic with SAFE_RADIUS to avoid player spawn, and dt‑scaled motion for each hazard.
7. Collision detection — Detect player‑hazard collisions, transition `phase` to "gameover", and halt gameplay updates.
8. Scoring — Increment score on defined events, render the numeric score at the top‑left of the canvas.
9. Rendering — Clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player (high contrast), and HUD elements each frame.
10. Game‑over overlay — Dim the screen, display "Game Over" with final score and instructions to press R or click Restart.
11. Observable contract — Populate `window.__game` each frame with `{ state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` where state is "playing" or "gameover" and objective is a short description.
12. Audio feedback — lazy‑initialize AudioContext in a beep() helper, wrap calls in try/catch, and trigger sounds on hit, score, and death events.

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Pulse Split</h1>
    <p class="game-description">A dot emits expanding rings; tap to split pulses and navigate obstacles for points.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap / Click to split the pulse</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>

    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>

  <script>



// === STATE =============================================================
// Define initial game state objects.
const player = { x: 400, y: 300, radius: 10, vx: 0, vy: 0 };
const hazards = []; // will hold obstacle objects
let score = 0;
let phase = "playing"; // "playing" | "gameover"
let objective = "Avoid the red hazards";
// Hazard spawn config
const SAFE_RADIUS = 150; // avoid spawning too close to player
let spawnTimer = 0;
const SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.5; // seconds
// === END STATE =========================================================

// === CONFIG =============================================================
const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
let loopId = null;
let lastTime = performance.now();
// Input tracking set
const keys = new Set();
// Lazy AudioContext
let audioCtx = null;
function getAudioContext() {
  if (!audioCtx) {
    try {
      audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('AudioContext init failed', e);
    }
  }
  return audioCtx;
}
function beep(freq = 440, duration = 0.1) {
  try {
    const ctx = getAudioContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.2, ctx.currentTime + 0.01);
    osc.start();
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + duration);
    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + duration);
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('beep failed', e);
  }
}
// === END CONFIG =========================================================

// === LOOP ===============================================================
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== "playing") return;
  // Player movement based on keys
  const accel = 800; // px/sec^2
  const maxSpeed = 400; // px/sec
  const friction = 0.9;
  let ax = 0, ay = 0;
  if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) ax -= accel;
  if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) ax += accel;
  if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) ay -= accel;
  if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) ay += accel;
  // apply acceleration
  player.vx = (player.vx || 0) + ax * dt;
  player.vy = (player.vy || 0) + ay * dt;
  // clamp speed
  const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
  if (speed > maxSpeed) {
    player.vx = (player.vx / speed) * maxSpeed;
    player.vy = (player.vy / speed) * maxSpeed;
  }
  // apply friction
  player.vx *= friction;
  player.vy *= friction;
  // update position
  player.x += player.vx * dt;
  player.y += player.vy * dt;
  // clamp within canvas
  player.x = Math.max(player.radius, Math.min(canvas.width - player.radius, player.x));
  player.y = Math.max(player.radius, Math.min(canvas.height - player.radius, player.y));

  // Increment score over time (1 point per second)
  const newScore = Math.floor(score + dt);
  if (newScore !== score) {
    beep(600);
  }
  score = newScore;

}

function renderPlayer() {
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

function renderBackground() {
  // solid background color
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
}

function renderHazards() {
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff4d4d"; // high contrast red
  for (const h of hazards) {
    if (!h.alive) continue;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
}

function renderHUD() {
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "16px sans-serif";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.textBaseline = "top";
  ctx.fillText(`Score: ${score}` , 10, 10);
}

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      // clear and redraw everything
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      renderBackground();
      renderHazards();
      renderPlayer();
      renderHUD();
      // Populate observable contract
      window.__game = {
        state: phase,
        score: score,
        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: phase === "playing" },
        objective: objective
      };
      if (phase === "gameover") {
        renderGameOver();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

function renderGameOver() {
  // dim overlay
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
  ctx.font = "32px sans-serif";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
  ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 30);
  ctx.font = "20px sans-serif";
  ctx.fillText(`Score: ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 10);
  ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 40);
}
// === END LOOP ===========================================================

// === INPUT ==============================================================
function reset() {
  // reinitialize game state
  player.x = 400; player.y = 300;
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  hazards.length = 0;
  keys.clear();
  startLoop();
}

document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
  keys.add(ev.key);
});
document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");
restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);
// === END INPUT ===========================================================

// Start the game
startLoop();

// Game script will be added in subsequent tasks.
  </script>
</body>
</html>

## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

(A game where neither happens has no progress signal — broken even if it runs.)\n197: \n198: ## Tips\n199: \n200: - Keep the implementation simple and robust over flashy and fragile.\n\n(Showing lines 1-200 of 202. Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Pulse Split
Concept: The dot continuously emits expanding rings; tapping splits the ring into two concentric pulses that can pass through nested obstacles, then recombine for a score boost.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.
